- **apex-ui-stylist**: APEX UI/CSS polish advisor for this service desk. Use when you want to improve the look of an APEX page/region/report — branded theme, dashboard tiles, card layouts, spacing, color, custom CSS overrides. Advisory: it outputs paste-ready CSS + where to put it; it does not edit the APEX app. ([Dhawi1902/servicedesk-apex](https://github.com/Dhawi1902/servicedesk-apex/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/apex-ui-stylist.md))

- **apex-worker**: Fable tier (strongest, most expensive). Only for the hardest roles: architecture-critical design with long-lived consequences, problems that already failed on Opus/deep-worker, subtle concurrency/security/correctness bugs, high-stakes reviews of a plan or critical code path, and final adjudication when Claude, GPT and Gemini lanes disagree.
